  ['GlowingMushroomPlants', 86], ['ExposedGemsInIceBiome', 91], ['ExposedGemsUnderground', 92],
]);
const DUMPNAME = new Map<number, string>();
const le16 = (p: string): Uint16Array => {
  const b = readFileSync(p);
  return new Uint16Array(b.buffer, b.byteOffset, b.length >> 1);
};
interface Ch { t: Uint16Array; a: Uint8Array; w: Uint16Array; l: Uint8Array; hf: Uint8Array; sl: Uint8Array; lt: Uint8Array; ia: Uint8Array }
const cache = new Map<number, Ch>();
function chans(idx: number): Ch | null {
  const hit = cache.get(idx);
  if (hit) return hit;
  const nm = DUMPNAME.get(idx);
  if (!nm) return null;
  const pre = `${G2}/${String(idx).padStart(3, '0')}_${nm}`;
  if (!existsSync(`${pre}.half`)) return null;
  const c: Ch = {
    t: le16(`${pre}.typ`), a: readFileSync(`${pre}.act`), w: le16(`${pre}.wal`), l: readFileSync(`${pre}.liq`),
    hf: readFileSync(`${pre}.half`), sl: readFileSync(`${pre}.slope`), lt: readFileSync(`${pre}.lty`), ia: readFileSync(`${pre}.inact`),
  };
  if (!NOCACHE) cache.set(idx, c);
  return c;
}

interface Row {
  idx: number; tag: string; dA: number; dT: number; dW: number; dL: number;
  dHf: number; dSl: number; dLt: number; dIa: number; dGh: number;
  first: string; pairs: Array<[string, number]>; samples: string[];
}
function cmp(tag: string, idx: number, st: {
  type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; wall: Uint16Array; liquid: Uint8Array;
  liquidType: Uint8Array; half: Uint8Array; slope: Uint8Array; wire: Uint8Array;
}): Row {

794	        for (let t = 0; t < 4; t++) {

797	          if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
798	          const ni = this.idx(nx, ny);
799	          if (st.liquid[ni] > 0 && st.liquidType[ni] === 3) this.addWater(nx, ny);

964	  // ================= 液体交互（Liquid.cs:888-1115 + GetLiquidMergeTypes 1385-1453） =================
965	  // 合并产物优先级（GetLiquidMergeTypes 逐段覆盖，后写胜出）：
966	  //   水 > 岩浆 > 蜂蜜 > 微光——任一方是微光 → Aetherium 块 659；
967	  //   否则 蜂蜜在场 → 229/230，只剩水岩组合 → 黑曜石 56
968	
969	  /** CreateLiquidMergeTile（Liquid.cs:1355-1384）：
970	   *  生成/读档期 → LiquidOverwriteStrip（整行液体类型统一，不落块）；
971	   *  运行期 → PlayLiquidChangeSound + ReplaceTile/KillTile+PlaceTile（掉落走 killTile 钩子） */
972	  private createMergeTile(x: number, y: number, tileType: number, thisType: number, mergeType: number) {
973	    const st = this.world.store;
974	    if (this.isGeneratingOrLoadingWorld && !this.createMergeTilesDuringGen) {
975	      // Liquid.cs:1360-1377：产物 → 源类型（229→水 230/56→岩浆 659→微光），
976	      // 地狱层强制岩浆；两处调用点目标格已预先清零 → strip 实为空操作，仅保留原版结构
977	      let stripType = -1;
978	      if (tileType === HONEY_BLOCK) stripType = 1;
979	      else if (tileType === CRISPY_HONEY || tileType === OBSIDIAN) stripType = 2;
980	      else if (tileType === AETHERIUM) stripType = 4;
981	      if (y >= st.h - 200) stripType = 2; // y >= UnderworldLayer
982	      if (stripType >= 0) this.liquidOverwriteStrip(x, y, stripType);
983	      return;
984	    }
985	    this.liquidChangeSound?.(x, y, thisType, mergeType); // WorldGen.PlayLiquidChangeSound
986	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
987	    if (st.flags[i]) this.killTileAt(x, y); // ReplaceTile：KillTile_DropItems（火把/植物掉落）；生成收尾=尘掷
988	    st.setTile(x, y, tileType);
989	  }
990	
991	  /** LiquidOverwriteStrip（Liquid.cs:1338-1352）：从 (x,y) 起向两侧延伸，
992	   *  把同行连续液体段的类型统一为 stripType */
993	  private liquidOverwriteStrip(x: number, y: number, stripType: number) {
994	    const st = this.world.store;
995	    for (let nx = x; nx >= 0 && st.liquid[this.idx(nx, y)] > 0; nx--) st.liquidType[this.idx(nx, y)] = stripType;
996	    for (let nx = x; nx < st.w && st.liquid[this.idx(nx, y)] > 0; nx++) st.liquidType[this.idx(nx, y)] = stripType;
997	  }
998	
999	  /** 生成收尾全图 LiquidCheck(createMergeTilesDuringGen:true)（WorldGen.cs:22639-22650）：
1000	   *  常规生成期交互不落块（strip 分支），唯独收尾这一次允许把残余接触转成块。
1001	   *  ★原版此处对湿格直接调 Liquid.LiquidCheck(x,y,liquidType(),...)——岩浆格
1002	   *  不走 LavaCheck 包装（无 7×7 沙漠 blast 前导），故 t==2 以 desertBlast=false */
1003	  finalGenMergeCheck() {
1004	    const st = this.world.store;
1005	    this.createMergeTilesDuringGen = true;
1006	    try {
1007	      for (let y = 1; y < st.h - 1; y++) {
1008	        const row = y * st.w;
1009	        for (let x = 1; x < st.w - 1; x++) {
1010	          const i = row + x;
1011	          if (st.liquid[i] === 0) continue;
1012	          const t = st.liquidType[i];
1013	          if (t === 2) this.lavaCheck(x, y, false);
1014	          else if (t === 3) this.honeyCheck(x, y);
1015	          else if (t === 4) this.shimmerCheck(x, y);
1016	        }
1017	      }
1018	    } finally {
1019	      this.createMergeTilesDuringGen = false;
1020	    }
1021	  }
1022	
1023	  /** UndergroundDesertCheck（Liquid.cs:1217-1231）：(x,y) 周围 7×7 有地下沙漠墙
1024	   *  （187 沙岩墙 / 216 硬化沙墙）即真。dump027 实证本时点沙漠墙 18.5 万格。 */
1025	  private undergroundDesertCheck(x: number, y: number): boolean {
1026	    const st = this.world.store;
1027	    for (let j = y - 3; j <= y + 3; j++) {
1028	      for (let i = x - 3; i <= x + 3; i++) {
1029	        if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= st.w || j >= st.h) continue; // InWorld(fluff=0)
1030	        const wv = st.wall[this.idx(i, j)];
1031	        if (wv === 187 || wv === 216) return true;
1032	      }
1033	    }
1034	    return false;
1035	  }

1037	  /** 岩浆格与水/蜂蜜/微光接触 → 黑曜石 56 / 脆蜂蜜块 230 / Aetherium 659（阈值 24）。
1038	   *  desertBlast=false = 跳过头部沙漠分支（生成收尾全图检查口径，见分支注释） */
1039	  private lavaCheck(x: number, y: number, desertBlast = true) {

	private GenPassResult RunPass(GenPass pass)
	{
		if (!pass.Enabled)
		{
			return new GenPassResult
			{
				Name = pass.Name,
				Skipped = true
			};
		}
		Stopwatch stopwatch = Stopwatch.StartNew();
		Main.rand = new UnifiedRandom(_seed);
		_progress.Start(pass.Weight);
		try
		{
			pass.Apply(_progress, _configuration.GetPassConfiguration(pass.Name));
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			_controller.ReportException("Exception in Pass: " + pass.Name, ex);
		}
		_progress.End();
		return new GenPassResult
		{
			Name = pass.Name,
			DurationMs = (int)stopwatch.ElapsedMilliseconds,
			RandNext = WorldGen.genRand.Next()

      if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`T(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${vt} j${jt}`);
    }
    if (c.w[i]! !== st.wall[i]!) { r.dW++; mark(i); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`W(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.w[i]} j${st.wall[i]}`); }
    if (c.l[i]! !== st.liquid[i]!) { r.dL++; mark(i); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`L(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.l[i]} j${st.liquid[i]}`); }
    if (c.hf[i]! !== st.half[i]!) { r.dHf++; mark(i); const k = `H${c.hf[i]}>${st.half[i]}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`H(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.hf[i]} j${st.half[i]}`); }
    if (c.sl[i]! !== st.slope[i]!) { r.dSl++; mark(i); const k = `S${c.sl[i]}>${st.slope[i]}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`S(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.sl[i]} j${st.slope[i]}`); }
    // lty：仅 liquid>0 的格裁决（vanilla 0 基 ↔ JS +1 基）
    const vl = c.l[i]! > 0 ? c.lt[i]! + 1 : 0;
    const jl = st.liquid[i]! > 0 ? st.liquidType[i]! : 0;
    if (vl !== jl) { r.dLt++; mark(i); const k = `Y${vl}>${jl}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`Y(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${vl} j${jl} liq${c.l[i]}`); }
    const via = c.ia[i]! ? 1 : 0;
    const jia = (st.wire[i]! >>> 5) & 1;
    if (via !== jia) { r.dIa++; mark(i); if (r.samples.length < 8) r.samples.push(`I(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${via} j${jia}`); }
    // ghost（信息位）：非活性残留 type
    const vg = va ? 0 : c.t[i]!;
    const jg = ja ? 0 : (SHEET[st.type[i]!] >= 0 ? SHEET[st.type[i]!]! : 9999);
    if (vg !== jg) r.dGh++;
  }
  if (firstI >= 0) r.first = `(${firstI % W},${Math.floor(firstI / W)})`;
  r.pairs = [...pair.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 8).map(([k, n]) => [k, n] as [string, number]);
  return r;
}

const srcCache = new Map<string, string[]>();
function labelOf(file: string, line: number): string {
  let src = srcCache.get(file);
  if (!src) { try { src = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split('\n'); } catch { src = []; } srcCache.set(file, src); }
  for (let l = Math.min(line, src.length) - 1; l >= 0 && l >= line - 40; l--) {
    const m = src[l]!.match(/▶([^（(]+?)[（(\s]/);
    if (m) return m[1].trim();
  }
  return `${file.split('/').pop()}:${line}`;
}

(RUN ? it : it.skip)('JS pipeline 8-channel slot diff vs /tmp/sw-slp/g (0..53)', async () => {
  for (const f of readdirSync(VG)) {
    const m = f.match(/^(\d{3})_(.+)\.typ$/);
    if (m) DUMPNAME.set(Number(m[1]), m[2]);
  }
  console.log(`dump names: ${DUMPNAME.size} (8ch dir=${G2})`);
  const rows: Row[] = [];
  let curStore: Parameters<typeof cmp>[2] | null = null;
  const seen = new Set<number>();
  const unmapped = new Set<string>();
  const RNGp = RNG.prototype as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
  const oReseed = RNGp.reseed as (this: unknown, s: number) => void;
  RNGp.reseed = function (this: unknown, s: number) {
    if (curStore) {
      const stack = new Error().stack ?? '';
      for (const line of stack.split('\n')) {
        const m = line.match(/((?:\/|[\w./-]*)src\/[^\s()]+\.ts):(\d+):\d+/);
        if (m && !m[1].includes('/core/rng') && !m[1].includes('_mile8')) {
          const lab = labelOf(m[1], Number(m[2]));
          const idx = LABEL2DUMP.get(lab);
          if (idx !== undefined && !seen.has(idx)) {
            seen.add(idx);
            rows.push(cmp(lab, idx, curStore!));
          } else if (idx === undefined && !unmapped.has(lab)) {
            unmapped.add(lab);
            console.log(`  [unmapped-label] ${lab} @${m[1].replace(/^.*?(src\/)/, '$1')}:${m[2]}`);
          }
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    return oReseed.call(this, s);
  };
  const t0 = Date.now();
  try {
    try {
      await generateWorld({
        width: W, height: H, seedText: SEED, evil: EVIL as -1 | 0 | 1,
        onWorldPartial: (w, i) => {
          if (!curStore) curStore = w.store as never as typeof curStore;
          const idx = SLOT_END.get(i);
          if (idx === undefined || seen.has(idx)) return;
          seen.add(idx);
          rows.push(cmp(`槽末#${i}`, idx, w.store as never as typeof curStore));
        },
      });
    } catch (e) { throw e; }
  } finally { RNGp.reseed = oReseed; }
  rows.sort((a, b) => a.idx - b.idx);
  for (const r of rows) {
    const bad = r.dA || r.dT || r.dW || r.dL || r.dHf || r.dSl || r.dLt || r.dIa;
    console.log(`${String(r.idx).padStart(3)} ${DUMPNAME.get(r.idx) ?? '?'} ← ${r.tag.padEnd(22)} ` +
      `A=${String(r.dA).padStart(7)} T=${String(r.dT).padStart(7)} W=${String(r.dW).padStart(6)} L=${String(r.dL).padStart(6)} ` +
      `Hf=${String(r.dHf).padStart(6)} Sl=${String(r.dSl).padStart(6)} Lt=${String(r.dLt).padStart(6)} Ia=${String(r.dIa).padStart(4)} (Gh=${r.dGh}) ` +
      `${bad ? '✗ ' + r.first : '✓'}`);
    if (bad) {
      console.log(`     pairs: ${r.pairs.map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}`);
      console.log(`     samples: ${r.samples.join(' | ')}`);
    }
  }
  const firstBad = rows.find((r) => r.dA || r.dT || r.dW || r.dL || r.dHf || r.dSl || r.dLt || r.dIa);
  const firstGh = rows.find((r) => r.dGh);
  console.log(`\n★ 8通道首差边界: ${firstBad ? `#${String(firstBad.idx).padStart(3)} ${DUMPNAME.get(firstBad.idx)} @${firstBad.tag} ${firstBad.first} ${firstBad.pairs.slice(0, 4).map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}` : '无（0..31 全绿）'}`);
  console.log(`★ ghost 首差边界(信息位): ${firstGh ? `#${firstGh.idx} ${DUMPNAME.get(firstGh.idx)} gh=${firstGh.dGh}` : '无'}`);
  console.log(`done ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)}s`);
  writeFileSync(`/tmp/mile8_${SEED}.json`, JSON.stringify(rows));
}, 900_000);

c.t[i]! : 0;\n    const jt = ja ? (SHEET[st.type[i]!] ?? 9999) : 0;\n    if (vt !== jt) { d[1]++; if (!first) first = `T(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${vt} j${jt}`; bump(`T${vt}>${jt}`); if (samples.length < 8) samples.push(`T(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${vt} j${jt}`); // 后续 49 pass 不需要\n        }\n      },\n    });\n  } catch (e) { if (e !== sentinel) throw e; }\n  console.log(`PhaseA: gensolid=${gens!.size} waterLine=${wl} shimmer=(${shX},${shY}) exempt=${JSON.stringify(ex)}`);\n  if (STOP) {\n    writeFileSync(`${SNAP}-fx.bin`, fx!); writeFileSync(`${SNAP}-fy.bin`, fy!); writeFileSync(`${SNAP}-wire.bin`, wire!);\n    writeFileSync(`${SNAP}-meta.json`, JSON.stringify({ W, H, SEED, EVIL, gens: [...gens!.entries()], wl, shX, shY, ex }));\n    console.log('PhaseA snapshot saved (STOP=1)');\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // ---- 基线复核：JS 管线槽 9 末 vs dump049 ----\n  const c49 = gold(49);\n  {\n    const st = { flags: new Uint8Array(0), type: new Uint16Array(0) } as never as TileStore;\n    const dL = (() => { let n = 0; st.type[i] = 0; } else st.type[i] = jid;\n    st.flags[i] = c48.a[i]! & 1;\n    st.wall[i] = c48.w[i]!;\n    st.liquid[i] = c48.l[i]!;\n    st.liquidType[i] = c48.l[i]! > 0 ? c48.lt[i]! + 1 : 0;\n    st.half[i] = c48.hf[i]!;\n    st.slope[i] = c48.sl[i]!;\n  }\n  st.frameX.set(fx!);   tick(dtMs: number) {
    // 原版 Main.time 恒速 tick（1t/帧）：dayLengthMs=全周期 24min 时，0.625/0.375 的
    // 昼夜窗口自然得出 15min/9min——★勿再按段调速（曾除反导致昼夜缩水，探针抓回）
    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;

  }
  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > Clock.DAWN && this.timeOfDay < Clock.DUSK; }
  get dayFactor(): number {
    // 昼夜平滑系数：白天 1，夜晚 0.32（月光下地表仍可见，火把不再像贴在黑幕上），晨昏过渡
    const t = this.timeOfDay;
    if (t > Clock.DAWN + 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK - 0.04) return 1;
    if (t >= Clock.DUSK - 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK + 0.04) return 1 - (t - (Clock.DUSK - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
    if (t >= Clock.DUSK + 0.04 || t < Clock.DAWN - 0.04) return 0.32;
    return 0.32 + (t - (Clock.DAWN - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
  }

JS update() does NOT do this! But JS quickWater() sets `this.solidNP[this.id379] = 1` once — "Main.tileSolid[379]=true（原版持续不恢复）". Since quickWater runs before the rounds, 379 stays solid through... unless something flips it back. tilesIgnoreWater(false) at quickWater end — restores solidNPBackup which had 379 already =1 (set before backup). worldGenTilesIgnoreWater(false) at settle end — restores solidNPBackupWG — the WG backup was taken BEFORE 379 was set? Order in settle: worldGenTilesIgnoreWater(true) [backup] → quickWater [sets 379=1] → ... → worldGenTilesIgnoreWater(false) [restore → 379 back to original!]. In vanilla: UpdateLiquid head has tilesIgnoreWater(true), tail (false) — wait, what does tilesIgnoreWater(false) restore in vanilla? It restores SetBoulderSolidity(solid: true) — the boulder family (138,484,664,665,711-716) — NOT 379. 379 is only set by QuickWater/Update heads and never unset during settle (SetBoulderSolidity doesn't touch 379? Let me verify SetBoulderSolidity). And the per-Update `Main.tileSolid[379] = true` makes it robust.

In JS: `tilesIgnoreWater(ignoreSolids)`: when false → `solidNP.set(solidNPBackup)` — restores THE WHOLE ARRAY snapshot taken at the first tilesIgnoreWater(true) call... wait no — look:

```ts
private tilesIgnoreWater(ignoreSolids: boolean): void {
  if (ignoreSolids) {
    if (this.solidNPBackup) return;
    this.solidNPBackup = this.solidNP.slice();
    for (const id of this.boulderFamilyIds) this.solidNP[id] = 0;
    if (this.id546 >= 0) this.solidNP[this.id546] = 0;
  } else if (this.solidNPBackup) {
    this.solidNP.set(this.solidNPBackup);
    this.solidNPBackup = null;
  }
}
```

In quickWater: `this.solidNP[this.id379] = 1; this.tilesIgnoreWater(true); ... this.tilesIgnoreWater(false);`
- 379 set to 1 BEFORE backup → backup contains 379=1 → restore keeps 379=1. OK equivalent to vanilla (379 stays solid).
- updateLiquid: tilesIgnoreWater(true) → backup taken (with 379=1 already) → restore OK.

So JS 379 handling ≈ vanilla within settle (since quickWater runs first). Not the bug. Fine.

**Update body comparison** — the neighbor wake section:

Vanilla (non-lava branch): wakes lava neighbors if tile.lava() — note: `tile.lava()` on a neighbor with liquid==0? lava() reads the liquid TYPE bit — which persists even when liquid==0? 	public static void AddWater(int x, int y)
	{
		Tile tile = Main.tile[x, y];
		if (Main.tile[x, y] == null || tile.checkingLiquid() || x >= Main.maxTilesX - 5 || y >= Main.maxTilesY - 5 || x < 5 || y < 5 || tile.liquid == 0 || (tile.nactive() && Main.tileSolid[tile.type] && tile.type != 546 && !Main.tileSolidTop[tile.type]))
		{
			return;
		}
		if (numLiquid >= curMaxLiquid - 1)
		{
			LiquidBuffer.AddBuffer(x, y);
			return;
		}
		tile.checkingLiquid(checkingLiquid: true);
		tile.skipLiquid(skipLiquid: false);
		Main.liquid[numLiquid].kill = 0;
		Main.liquid[numLiquid].x = x;
		Main.liquid[numLiquid].y = y;
		Main.liquid[numLiquid].delay = 0;
		numLiquid++;
		if (Main.netMode == 2)
		{
			NetSendLiquid(x, y);
		}
		if (!tile.active() || WorldGen.isGeneratingOrLoadingWorld)
		{
			return;
		}
		bool flag = false;
		if (tile.lava())
		{
			if (TileObjectData.CheckLavaDeath(tile))
			{
				flag = true;
			}
		}
		else if (TileObjectData.CheckWaterDeath(tile))
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		if (flag)
		{
			WorldGen.KillTile(x, y);
			if (Main.netMode == 2)
			{
				NetMessage.SendData(17, -1, -1, null, 0, x, y);
			}
		}
	}

if(jl[i]>0&&vl[i]===0)jsOnly++;\n    vanDelta+=vl[i]-v48[i]; jsDelta+=jl[i]-v48[i];\n    if(vt[i]===1)water++;else if(vt[i]===2||vt[i]===3||vt[i]===4)lava++;\n    for(let dy=-1;dy<=1;dy++)for(let dx=-1;dx<=1;dx++){\n      if(!dx&&!dy)continue;\n      const nx=x+dx,ny=y+dy;\n      if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=W||ny>=H)continue;\n      const j=ny*W+nx;\n      if(diff[j]&&!seen[j]){seen[j]=1;if(tail<q.length)q[tail++]=j;}\n    }\n  }\n  comps.push({cnt,minX,maxX,minY,maxY,vanOnly,jsOnly,vanDelta,jsDelta,water});\n}\ncomps.sort((a,b)=>b.cnt-a.cnt);\nconsole.log(`components: ${comps.length}`);\nfor(const c of comps.slice(0,30)){\n  console.log(`  cnt=${String(c.cnt).padStart(6)} x[${c.minX},${c.maxX}] y[${c.minY},${c.maxY}] vanOnlyCells=${c.vanOnly} jsOnlyCells=${c.jsOnly} Δvan=${c.vanDelta} Δjs=${c.jsDelta} waterCells=${c.water}`);\n}\nEOF\nnode /tmp/d5-cc.mjs",

~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/LiquidBuffer.cs
namespace Terraria;

public class LiquidBuffer
{
	public static int numLiquidBuffer;

	public int x;

	public int y;

	public static void AddBuffer(int x, int y)
	{
		if (numLiquidBuffer < 49998 && !Main.tile[x, y].checkingLiquid())
		{
			Main.tile[x, y].checkingLiquid(checkingLiquid: true);
			Main.liquidBuffer[numLiquidBuffer].x = x;
			Main.liquidBuffer[numLiquidBuffer].y = y;
			numLiquidBuffer++;
		}
	}

	public static void DelBuffer(int l)
	{
		numLiquidBuffer--;
		Main.liquidBuffer[l].x = Main.liquidBuffer[numLiquidBuffer].x;
		Main.liquidBuffer[l].y = Main.liquidBuffer[numLiquidBuffer].y;
	}
}

```

WAIT — order matters! Vanilla: 
```csharp

It reads buffer[0] (x,y), clears checking, AddWater, THEN DelBuffer(0) — the swap-remove happens AFTER AddWater.   this.bufY[0] = this.bufY[this.bufCount];
}

private updateStalactiteStyleGen(x: number, j: number): boolean {
  // Main.netMode==1 门不适用（生成期 netMode=0）
  const g1 = this.getStalactiteStyleGen(x, j);          // {style, fail}

  if (g2.fail) return false;
  if (g1.style !== g2.desiredStyle) {

      case 0: num += 54; break;
      case 1: num += 216; break;
      case 2: num += 270; break;
      case 3: num += 324; break;
      case 4: num += 378; break;

      case 9: num += 594; break;
      case 10: num += 648; break;
      case 11: num += 108; break;
      case 12: num += 162; break;
    }
    for (let i = g2.y; i < g2.y + g2.height; i++) st.frameX[this.idx(x, i)] = num;
  }
  return true;
}

private checkBannerGen(x: number, j: number, type: number): void {

	public static void TileFrame(int i, int j, bool resetFrame = false, bool noBreak = false)
	{
		if (i <= 5 || j <= 5 || i >= Main.maxTilesX - 5 || j >= Main.maxTilesY - 5 || Main.tile[i, j] == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		MapUpdateQueue.Add(i, j);
		try
		{
			Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
			if (!tile.active())
			{
				tile.halfBrick(halfBrick: false);
				tile.ClearBlockPaintAndCoating();
				tile.slope(0);
			}
			if (tile.liquid > 0 && Main.netMode != 1 && !generatingWorld)
			{
				Liquid.AddWater(i, j);
			}
			if (!tile.active())
			{
				return;
			}
			int type = tile.type;
			if (Main.tileFrameImportant[type])
			{
				if (!noBreak || TileID.Sets.Torches[type] || TileID.Sets.FrameImportantEchoCulling[type])
				{
					TileFrameImportant(i, j, type, tile, resetFrame);
				}
				return;
			}

private checkStalactiteGen(x: number, j: number): void {
  const st = this.world.store;
  if (x < 0 || j < 0 || x >= st.w || j >= st.h) return;   // cs:39024 null 门
  const i0 = this.idx(x, j);
  let num = j;
  const fy0 = st.frameY[i0]!;
  if (fy0 === 72) {
    // 悬挂单格（cs:39027-39051）：上方 SolidTile 支撑 + 样式维护，败=杀本格

this.idx(x, num) : -1, ib = inB ? this.idx(x, num + 1) : -1;\n      let bad = false;\n      if (!this.solidTileFull(x, num + 2)) bad = true;               // cs:39119\n      if (!inA || !inB || st.flags[ia] === 0 || st.flags[ib] === 0\n        || st.type[ib] !== st.type[ia]\n        || st.frameX[ib] !== st.frameX[ia]) bad = true;              // cs:39123-39131\n      if (!bad && this.invalidTileForPilesGen(x, num + 2)) bad = true;\n      if (!bad && !this.updateStalagtiteStyleGen(x, num)) bad = true;\n      if (bad) {\n        this.destroyObj = true;\n        if (inA && st.type[ia] === ty) this.killTileGen(x, num);\n        if (inB && st.type[ib] === ty) this.killTileGen(x, num + 1);\n        this.destroyObj = false;\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n    // frameY ∈ {0,18}：悬挂 2 格（cs:39138-39189）\n    if (fy0 === 18) num--;\n    const inA = num >= 0 && num < st.h, inB = num + 1 >= 0 && num + 1 < st.h;\n    const ia = inA ? this.idx(x, num) : -1, ib = inB ? this.idx(x, num + 1) : -1;\n    let bad = false;\n    if (!this.solidTileFull(x, num - 1)) bad = true;                 // cs:39173\n    if (!inA || !inB || st.flags[ia] === 0 || st.flags[ib] === 0\n      || st.type[ib] !== st.type[ia]\n      || st.frameX[ib] !== st.frameX[ia]) bad = true;\n    if (!bad && !this.updateStalagtiteStyleGen(x, num)) bad = true;\n    if (bad) {\n      this.destroyObj = true;\n      if (inA && st.type[ia] === ty) this.killTileGen(x, num);\n      if (inB && st.type[ib] === ty) this.killTileGen(x, num + 1);\n      this.destroyObj = false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** CheckBanner（cs:86442 派发 → WorldGen.CheckBanner）：1×3 竖排旗帜，\n   *  frameY/18 反解顶格 + 3 格组帧校验 + 上方锚（nactive + 实心族门 + 平台特例\n   *  380/Platforms）→ 任一破整组杀。tileSolid/tileSolidTop/Platforms 用\n   *  solid||platform / platform 近似（UUU 批同口径：平台族 vanilla 双 true） */\n  private checkBannerGen(x: number, j: number, type: number): void {\n    if (this.destroyObj) return;                                     // cs:86442 头闸\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    const i0 = this.idx(x, j);\n    let row = Math.trunc((st.frameY[i0] ?? 0) / 18);\n    let num2 = 0;\n    while (row >= 3) { row -= 3; num2++; }                           // 样式段（3 行/样式）\n    const num = j - row;                                             // 组顶格 y\n    const frameX = st.frameX[i0] ?? 0;\n    let bad = false;\n    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {\n      const yy = num + k;\n      if (yy < 0 || yy >= st.h) { bad = true; continue; }            // 越界=新 Tile→失配\n      const q = this.idx(x, yy);\n      if (st.flags[q] === 0 || st.type[q] !== type\n        || st.frameY[q] !== k * 18 + num2 * 54\n        || st.frameX[q] !== frameX) bad = true;                      // cs:86452-86464\n    }\n    const ay = num - 1;\n    if (ay < 0 || ay >= st.h) bad = true;                            // 锚越界=新 Tile\n    else {\n      const q = this.idx(x, ay);\n      const act = st.flags[q] !== 0;\n      if (!act) bad = true;                                          // cs:86469 !nactive\n      else {\n        const t = st.type[q]!;\n        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];\n        const topSlope = st.slope[q] === 1 || st.slope[q] === 2;\n        const halfBrick = st.half[q] !== 0;\n        const bottomSlope = st.slope[q] === 3 || st.slope[q] === 4;\n        const tileSolid = !!(d && (d.solid || d.platform));          // vanilla tileSolid\n        const solidTop = !!d?.platform;                              // ≈ tileSolidTop\n        const isPlatform = !!d?.platform;                            // ≈ Sets.Platforms\n        const num3 = topSlope || halfBrick || tileSolid;             // cs:86471\n        const flag2 = !solidTop || (isPlatform && (halfBrick || topSlope));\n        let flag3 = num3 && flag2\n          && !NOT_REALLY_SOLID_SHEETS.has(SHEET_OF[t] ?? -1) && !bottomSlope;\n        if (isPlatform || SHEET_OF[t] === 380) flag3 = true;         // cs:86479 平台/花盆箱特例\n        if (!flag3) bad = true;                                      // cs:86481\n      }\n    }\n    if (!bad) return;\n    this.destroyObj = true;\n    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {\n      const yy = num + k;\n      if (yy < 0 || yy >= st.h) continue;\n      if (st.type[this.idx(x, yy)] === type) this.killTileGen(x, yy);\n    }\n    this.destroyObj = false;\n    // cs:86491+ 的 type==91 掉落支（Item.NewItem 5673/...）：生成期不产实体，备案\n  }"
}

## DDDDD 批（2026-08-19 13:10-14:30）：m 链 #49 湖体 L=11,707 清零——LiquidBuffer 回灌双重错位（中世界首触 25000 帽）+ TileFrameImportant 165/91 派发补齐 + oceanDepths 头扫

### ⓪ 复现与反事实定位
- m 链 #49 复现：L=11707 Lt=16 A=2 T=2 首差 (4775,198)（v6 j8）。
- 独立重放器（_d5rep，golden048 八通道+JS frame/wire/GenSolid 界 + RNG(seed) pass 流）：复现同值 11707 → settle 纯自因（#48 输入无辜）。
- 连通域分析：11,707 差 = **475 条全部单行薄膜带**（湖面 evaporation 薄膜：y198 天空湖 68 格 v6/j8、y370 海面 272 格 v141/j136……）——范围一致、均匀度一致、高度差 → 全局调度差而非局部规则差。
- 阶段化重放：薄膜全部在 r0（774 次 updateLiquid）成形；**r0 期活动列表顶到 curMaxLiquid-1=24999，LiquidBuffer 首次介入（maxBuf=1563，介入 70 个分片周期）**——小世界永不触帽 = 四链中唯中世界翻车的原因。

### ① 根因一：LiquidBuffer 回灌双重错位（LiquidSim.ts）
- 原版（Liquid.cs:1134-1148，1405 :793-806 双版一致）：
  - 回灌量 num8 = `curMaxLiquid - (curMaxLiquid - numLiquid)` = **numLiquid**（当前活动数），非空余量；
  - `LiquidBuffer.DelBuffer(0)` = **swap-remove**（num--; buf[0]=buf[num]——尾元素补头位）→ 出序 A,C,B…（非 FIFO）；
  - 顺序：先 checking 清零 → AddWater → **后** DelBuffer(0)（满载时 AddBuffer 尾接同格 + swap 换回头位 = 同格空转）。
- JS 曾实现：head/tail 环形 FIFO + 回灌量 min(curMaxLiquid-numLiquid, bufLen)（空余量）→ 介入期回灌时序整体漂移 → 湖面薄膜混沌高度全漂。
- 修法：bufCount 计数 + 尾接 + swap-remove（LiquidBuffer.cs 12-29 镜像）；O(1) 无压缩（旧 O(n²) 教训保持成立）。
- 战果：重放器 dL49 11707→**0**；管线 #49 L/Lt 全绿。

### ② 根因二：TileFrameImportant 派发缺 165/91（YYY 级联扩员）
- m 链残 A=2/T=2 (4260,1161)：蛛网水杀（51 水死表）→ KillTile 尾 SquareTileFrame 3×3 → TileFrame(165) → CheckStalactite（cs:85996→39022）：悬挂 2 格组支撑 (4260,1160) 空 → vanilla 杀 (4260,1161/1162)，JS tileFrameGen 只派发 484/485。
- s22222 残 A=2/T=2 (2134,1082)：旗帜底格先亡 → cs:86442→CheckBanner 组帧校验破 → vanilla 杀整组 1×3，JS 无派发。
- 修法：tileFrameGen +`case 165 → checkStalactiteGen`（cs:39022-39189 四分支 + UpdateStalagtiteStyle 族 cs:38669-38899：style≠desired 耗 Next(3) 走 pass 链流）+ `case 91 → checkBannerGen`（cs:86442 派发；锚门 tileSolid≈solid||platform / tileSolidTop/Platforms≈platform / NotReallySolid{387,388,10}）。余成员维持 YYY 备案。
- 战果：m 链 #49 八通道全零；连带 **#50-#57 全绿 + #53 Smooth_World 旧 Hf578/Sl2375 半砖债整段消失**（其为 #49 液体差的下游——薄膜修正后唇缘/半砖判定全对齐）。

### ③ 附带补齐：oceanDepths 头扫（settle.ts）
- 原版 #49 pass 头（cs:16222-16229）全图 oceanDepths 带（y≤oceanLevel=(ws+rl)/2+40 且 x<380||x>W-380）非水液体归水——**唯 #49 有**（#97 cs:21051 无；QuickCleanup cs:18005 自有窗口版 JS 已有）。settleWorldLiquids +oceanStrip 参（liquidSettlePass 传 true；worker 尾部二次沉降缺省 false）。
- 四链现值该域无非水液体（m 链海洋带岩浆全在 y≥1212 > oceanLevel≈630）→ 惰性 1:1，四链复核零变化。

### ④ 验证
- m 链：首差 #49 → **#58 Statues (3612,662) T0>165×3**（#49-#57 全绿；#58=Statues/BuriedChests 域存量债新暴露，非本批域）；s 链：首差 #49 → **#58 Statues (3303,372)**。

DDDDD 批次完成。最终报告：

## 战果总览

**任务达成**：m20260811 链 #49 湖体 L=11,707 **清零**（八通道全绿），s22222 #49 旗 2 格同案清零，四链零回退。

### 首分叉定罪（两级根因，均落在 settle 自身）

**根因一（主）— LiquidBuffer 回灌双重错位**（vanilla Liquid.cs:1134-1148 + LiquidBuffer.cs:12-29，1405/1456 双版一致）：
- 回灌量 = `curMaxLiquid-(curMaxLiquid-numLiquid)` = **numLiquid**（当前活动数），JS 曾用空余量 `min(curMaxLiquid-numLiquid, bufLen)`；
- `DelBuffer(0)` = **swap-remove**（尾元素补头位，出序 A,C,B…），JS 曾为 FIFO 环形队列；弹出时序也在 AddWater 之后（满载时同格空转语义）。

**触发条件实锤**（独立重放器 + 阶段化打点）：中世界 r0 唤醒链把活动列表顶到 24999 帽，缓冲器**首次介入**（maxBuf=1563、70 个分片周期）——小世界永不触帽 = 三条小链 #49 长绿、唯中世界翻车的结构性原因。11,707 差格 = **475 条湖面薄膜带**（范围/形状两侧全同、唯圆整漂移方向不同 = 全局调度差指纹）。修复后重放器 dL 11707→**0**。
